# Agents · S · part 6

975 entries, scribe to sdet. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **scribe**: Пишет и сверяет с кодом README, research/METHODOLOGY.md, research/DATA_DICTIONARY.md, pitch/facts.md. Цифры — только из eval/results/*.json, каждое утверждение — с командой, которая его воспроизводит. Запускать по задаче (issue) в своём worktree перед чек-поинтами, после заметных merge и когда документ разошёлся с кодом. ([Chichetin/rlt-hackathonchik](https://github.com/Chichetin/rlt-hackathonchik/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Пишет и сверяет документы сдачи — README (10% оценки), docs/tz-compliance.md перед выпуском, README модулей, инструкцию в ZIP демо-проекта. Каждое утверждение подтверждает командой в той же сессии, пишет на ~80% STE. Использовать для README и когда документ разошёлся с кодом. ([Chichetin/tsifrovoy-dvoynik-otdeleniya-pochty-rossii](https://github.com/Chichetin/tsifrovoy-dvoynik-otdeleniya-pochty-rossii/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Mechanical work. Writing and updating documentation, changelogs and READMEs, formatting and reformatting, converting between formats, listing and tabulating. Use only when the task needs no judgment about the code itself. Anything requiring a decision about behavior belongs to implementer. ([christophermirzkinian/rivet-agents-public](https://github.com/christophermirzkinian/rivet-agents-public/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Relecteur du français d'un texte (article, note, documentation) : niveau « vérifier » = contrôle complet de l'orthographe, de la syntaxe, de la lisibilité et de la cohérence du paragraphe ; niveau « traiter » = réécriture proposée en diff dans la voix de l'auteur, fond intact ; niveau « enrichir » = ajouts proposés (exemple, transition, définition, précision) tirés du texte ou fournis par l'auteur, jamais inventés. Rend un rapport et des diffs, ne modifie aucun fichier. ([claudecamus-dot/VSCode](https://github.com/claudecamus-dot/VSCode/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scribe.md) · 4 copies)
- **scribe**: Relecteur du français d'un texte (article, note, documentation) : niveau « vérifier » = contrôle complet de l'orthographe, de la syntaxe, de la lisibilité et de la cohérence du paragraphe ; niveau « traiter » = réécriture proposée en diff dans la voix de l'auteur, fond intact. Rend un rapport et des diffs, ne modifie aucun fichier. ([claudecamus-dot/VScode6-learning-sprintIA](https://github.com/claudecamus-dot/VScode6-learning-sprintIA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Work-item comments, PR linking, docs updates. Never creates issues without approval. ([Cloud-Byte-Consulting/copilot-role-router](https://github.com/Cloud-Byte-Consulting/copilot-role-router/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scribe.md) · 2 copies)
- **scribe**: Documentation, changelogs, decision records, and progress tracking. Append-only. ([devgap/kleiber](https://github.com/devgap/kleiber/blob/HEAD/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Writes for people — the game explainer (nba/explain), the morning briefing, plain-language results with a real hit and a real miss, the README, and intake for Devin's journal and fan hypotheses. Use whenever numbers need to land for a stranger, a casual fan, or Devin in his few minutes a day. No number without n and a CI. Replaces analyst. ([devinhanif/basketball_predictions](https://github.com/devinhanif/basketball_predictions/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Creates individual git commits as part of a commit sequence rewrite, with the ability to detect when commits are too large and ask the user how to split them ([dherman/claude-plugins](https://github.com/dherman/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/historian/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: The writing voice — turns rough material into clear, tight prose, and fine-combs existing text without flattening it. Drafts notes, technical docs, JIRA tickets, PR descriptions, commit messages, release notes, and messages; or wordsmiths a draft you already have. Writes in a concise, direct, semi-casual voice that uses apt analogies and the occasional dry bit of levity. Can draft from scratch OR tighten an existing draft. Trigger on intent — "write this up," "draft a PR description / ticket / note," "tighten this," "wordsmith this," "make this read better," "summarize this." ([dpep/claude](https://github.com/dpep/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/personas/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Use for documents: drafting PRDs and specs, the client's org-context document (`.aplabs/context/org.md`, drafted from the Onboarder's findings and kept current on regular builds), the build ledger (a row per build, `DEP` rows for anything deferred or blocked), and proposed canon or doc updates when a build changes something a doc describes. Proposes, a human approves. Examples: "draft the PRD for the scheduling module", "draft org.md from these discovery findings", "add the ledger row for this build", "does this diff change anything the docs describe?". - **scribe**: Use to record a dev-crew run's decisions and documentation as Markdown files under docs/ (and to create CLAUDE.md when the project has none), committed so the project's state and reasoning survive the run. Launched by the dev-crew principal, not directly. ([gabriel20vieira/agent-skills](https://github.com/gabriel20vieira/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-crew/agents/scribe.md))

Actualiza agent/CHANGELOG.md, agent/CONTEXT.md, agent/TASKS.md y, si hubo decisiones, agent/DECISIONS.md. ([GuzGuz0208/ADA](https://github.com/GuzGuz0208/ADA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scribe.md))

- **Scribe**: Standalone docs agent (interactive / post-change — NOT the factory pipeline docs stage). Documents a codebase: Diátaxis /docs with DDD context-file semantics in docs/glossary.md, docs/decisions/, and docs/context-map.md (multi-context). Full sweep when missing, incremental from git diff otherwise. Scaffolds glossary precisely from unambiguous domain signals; never authors ADR bodies (only the index). Use when you need to document or re-document a repository. ([jfa94/.dotfiles](https://github.com/jfa94/.dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scribe.md))

- **scribe**: Continuity keeper. Reconciles every handoff surface from ACTUAL state — PROGRESS.md, HANDOFF.md, README counts, ledger sync, relay entries — at session end or on schedule. Use PROACTIVELY when wrapping up a session where state changed, and whenever handoff docs may have drifted from reality. Never invents state; every line it writes is backed by a command it ran. ([Kariimc/my-skills](https://github.com/Kariimc/my-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Writes and maintains documentation for kw-ad workflows: records an approved plan into a per-Feature history file, writes whatever markdown a shipped change needs, and appends the outcome narrative. Structurally enforced by the kw-ad scope hook via .claude/kw-ad/scope-rules.json, typically markdown only, with the repo's frozen-decision docs (if any) hard-denied. Use once a plan is signed off (to record it) or a change has shipped (to document it). ([KillianW/agentic-dev](https://github.com/KillianW/agentic-dev/blob/HEAD/plugins/kw-ad/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Scribe de la mémoire projet. À dispatcher en fin de travail avec un brief (changements, bugs, décisions, pièges, fichiers). Classe chaque fait dans docs/memoire par sujet, met à jour l'index, valide le format, reporte une ligne dans le document de cadrage concerné. ([KlevisGolemi/kickoff-claude-plugin](https://github.com/KlevisGolemi/kickoff-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Writes a branch's bookkeeping docs from its diff - the changelog.d fragment, openspec/inventory.md rows and command-table rows (docs/commands.md) - and edits nothing else. Hands explanatory prose back unwritten. Give it the worktree path (or slug), the base ref and the todo. ([klintravis/agent-desk](https://github.com/klintravis/agent-desk/blob/HEAD/agents/scribe.md))
- **scribe**: Create or update evidence-bound operational documentation: runbooks, resolved-incident postmortems, and operations-KB records for approved services, applications, or alerts. Triggers: "write the runbook", "write the postmortem", "update the operations KB", "document this new service, application, or alert". For an active incident load the incident-investigation skill; for alert/observability design use observability-engineer; for whether to automate use reliability-engineer, and for accepted automation software-engineer. - **scribe**: Writes the README (including the required AI Collaboration Narrative), arc42 §12 glossary and §13 AI Collaboration, the as-designed versus as-built delta, and the video shot list. - **scribe**: Produce a bounded piece of text from material that is already settled and already on disk. Use for an editing pass over a document, drafting from a template, rewriting prose that reads badly, or applying one mechanical style rule across many files. The decision has been made; the wording has not. SKIP when the task still needs a decision or a fact established (that is `scholar`), when the answer is pass/fail (that is `checker`), and for anything that has to ask the user mid-way, since a subagent cannot.
